It controls epistemic status and containment, never evidence visibility or presumptive acceptance/rejection.","legacy_docket_assessment":{"adjudicable":false,"packet":null,"status":"invalid"},"packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity":[{"focus_ref":"1:3","identity_status":"legacy_unbound","packet_identity":null,"protocol":"focus-trace-hermetic-response-v4","reader_id":"reader_hft_a","trace_kind":"reconstructed"}]},"reader_synthesis_count":10,"source_present":true,"structured_insight_count":13,"unstructured_record_count":0},"identity":{"ayah_ref":"1:3","lane":"global","linguistic_source_ref":"1:3","surface_ref":"1:3","unit_kind":"numbered_ayah"},"primary_floor":{"source_ref":"1:3","target_tokens":[["Merhameti",["1:3:1"]],["sınırsızdır",["1:3:1"]],["merhamet",["1:3:2"]],["edendir",["1:3:2"]]],"text":"Merhameti sınırsızdır, merhamet edendir."},"schema_version":"commentary-v5-hermetic-scope-packet-v1","scope":{"hft":{"assigned_record_count":10,"authoring_policy":{"every_assigned_record_requires_explicit_review":true,"parseable_hft_is_always_visible_to_authoring":true,"provenance_affects_qualification_not_visibility":true,"scope_mismatch_affects_lane_assignment_not_visibility":true,"unresolved_branch_citations_do_not_suppress_records":true},"authoring_status":"visible_with_provenance_qualification"},"lane_contract":"reader walks and cross-run publications with explicit trust labels","pericope":{"ayah_from":1,"ayah_to":7,"id":"s001-p01-001-007","label":"Whole surah","number":1,"refs":["1:1","1:2","1:3","1:4","1:5","1:6","1:7"]},"readiness":{"authoring_effect":"Docket readiness is reported for source provenance. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1b35e99c2fb5e1a76a56","text":"The later negative pair in 1:7 made 1:3 more dynamic. “Merciful” became not only benevolent but anti-hardening: it stands before the hard compactness of غضب and the disappearance/loss of ضلال as a preserving, softening force.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001","root_000552/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1c4085b648f2357542f9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:3:1\",\"root_000552\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"1:3:2\",\"root_000552\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yinelenen merhamet, bağımlı olanı koruyan bir oluşum alanını ve yol boyunca süren desteği birlikte görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1c9b76b0644df6382e0f","text":"3. **Exploratory: mercy includes vulnerable after-birth repair.** Evidence: `ر ح م B004` womb ailment after birth, activated by `ر ب ب B009` animal just after birth/freshness and `ر ب ب B002` stage-by-stage nurture, with later `ه د ي B008` supported walking. Mechanism: this is a weaker but visible material analogy: the surah moves from birth-like invocation into supported walking on a path; mercy includes the painful vulnerability after emergence. The packet supplies postpartum ailment, freshness after birth, nurture, and supported walking; I infer the lifecycle arc. Abductive move: if guidance is requested after naming and praise, the speaker may be newly born into dependence rather than already stable. Reading change: mercy becomes restorative care for fragile transition, not only benevolent feeling.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2dde0b803f410fe142b1","text":"1:4 made the placement of the fixed pair newly significant: mercy does not follow judgment as repair but precedes it as the medium in which sovereignty is announced. More surprisingly, the collective grammar of 1:5–1:6 and the predecessor-defined road of 1:7 activated `ر ح م` B002 as affiliation across recipients; this corrected an initial tendency to treat the two mercy forms only as intensified near-synonyms.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_33bf02bc7dc7c085ec7a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:3:1\",\"root_000552\",[\"B001\"]],[\"1:3:2\",\"root_000552\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İki merhamet adı, yetiştirme ile hesap arasında durarak yönetimin işleyişini merhametin içinden geçirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_38b677bedfb7cb3c721c","text":"The later “إِيَّاكَ” and “اهدنا” made the kinship/womb branch less decorative: mercy gathers a “we” before the path is requested. 1:7 also sharpened the contrast; mercy is the alternative atmosphere to both `غ ض ب B001` retaliatory anger and `ض ل ل B001/B003` loss of aim or owner.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_508af9abd6c2644e61a0","text":"1. **Mercy as the governing medium between nurture and reckoning.** **Branch evidence:** both fixed words instantiate `ر ح م` B001, tenderness, compassion, and beneficence toward the recipient. **Structural cue:** the pair stands immediately after 1:2 `رَبِّ` and immediately before 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ يَوْمِ ٱلدِّينِ`; the same pair has already appeared in 1:0. **Role and abductive move:** the packet supplies the placement and repetition; I infer that the intervening mercy pair qualifies how nurture, authority, and reckoning cohere. **Reading change:** mercy becomes the operating mode of rule rather than an isolated affective attribute set beside rule.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7828b6bec44e8748ee2a","text":"3. **Mercy as softening force against later hardening.** Lexical evidence: ر ح م B001, tenderness; ن ع م B002 in 1:7, softness/easeful living; غ ض ب B004/B005 in 1:7, hard compact rock and thick intense redness. Structural cue: tenderness and mercy precede the negative images of anger and deviation. Assigned role: mercy supplies material softness before the packet later shows hardened wrath. Abductive move: the packet supplies tenderness/softness and hardness/anger; I infer a material contrast in the moral landscape. Reading change: the ayah reads as a softening condition that keeps the path from becoming only a hard juridical surface.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B005","root_000552/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8ae647e5614106c3a243","text":"2. **Mercy as kinship-making around the praying “we.”** Evidence: `ر ح م B002` kinship bond and `ر ح م B003` womb; activated by the first-person plural in 1:5–1:6 and by “الذين أنعمت عليهم” in 1:7; supported by `ر ب ب B005` fostered child/step-family relation. Mechanism: the later “we/us” and “those upon whom” form a social body; mercy supplies the bond by which speakers and favored predecessors can belong together. The packet supplies kinship, womb, foster-care, and collective pronouns; I infer the family-making relation. Abductive move: a group seeking one path needs more than instruction; it needs a bond that makes shared guidance possible. Reading change: the repeated divine mercy reads as incorporation into a protected relation, not only as compassion toward isolated individuals.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B002","root_000552/B003","root_001525/B001","root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_96f1019255dfb30db631","text":"2. **Mercy as kinship-like continuity across the prayer.** Lexical evidence: ر ح م B002, kinship bond; ر ح م B003, womb; ع ب د B003 in 1:5, worship/submissive obedience; ه د ي B004 in 1:6, gracious gift; ن ع م B001 in 1:7, favor. Structural cue: the prayer moves from divine names to “we worship,” “guide us,” and “those You favored.” Assigned role: mercy supplies the relational continuity that lets command, help, guidance, and favor belong to one household of dependence. Abductive move: the packet supplies kinship/womb, worship, gift, and favor; I infer a dependency-system rather than isolated attributes. Reading change: the double mercy reads as the generative bond within which the worshipping “we” can speak.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9a3d6c2de50a13858501","text":"1. **Mercy repeated as bridge between cosmic lordship and legal reckoning.** Lexical evidence: ر ح م B001, mercy and beneficence; ر ب ب B001/B002 in 1:2, authority and nurture; د ي ن B002 in 1:4, reckoning/recompense. Structural cue: this ayah repeats 1:1’s mercy immediately after “Lord of the worlds” and immediately before “Owner of the Day of dīn.” Assigned role: mercy is the hinge that prevents lordship from reading as bare power and reckoning from reading as bare penalty. Abductive move: the packet supplies the adjacency; I infer that repetition performs a framing function across the transition. Reading change: the ayah reads as a structural brace in the surah’s sequence, not only as a restated attribute.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b31a34cf08b5479103be","text":"3. **Mercy as a bond that forms a path-community.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ر ح م` B002 is close kinship and the maintenance of kin ties. **Structural cue:** 1:5–1:6 speak in a collective first person, and 1:7 defines the requested road by relation to people already favored rather than by geometry alone. **Role and abductive move:** kinship supplies the social bond, while the collective petition and predecessor-defined path supply its structure; exploratorily, I infer that shared reception of mercy and favor creates affiliation across the two groups. **Reading change:** the pair can establish relational continuity between present petitioners and earlier path-bearers, not only kindness to separate individuals.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bb79d1becda69e351bfd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:3:1\",\"root_000552\",[\"B002\"]],[\"1:3:2\",\"root_000552\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Merhamet, birlikte dua edenlerle daha önce iyilik görmüş yolcular arasında yakınlık kuran bir topluluk bağına dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c0a8cb84b2bf462fd8d9","text":"1. **Mercy as the hinge between nurture and reckoning.** Evidence: `ر ح م B001` mercy/tenderness, preceded by `ر ب ب B002` nurture/completion in 1:2 and followed by `م ل ك B003` kingship plus `د ي ن B002` reckoning in 1:4. Mechanism: the repeated mercy sits structurally between ربّ care and ملك يوم الدين, preventing either side from being read alone. The packet supplies the sequence; I infer the mediating function. Abductive move: an attribute repeated at the transition point is meant to govern both what came before and what comes after. Reading change: the ayah becomes a hinge that carries tenderness into sovereignty and sovereignty back into care.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ca792553b569d4b2c64d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:3:1\",\"root_000552\",[\"B004\"]],[\"1:3:2\",\"root_000552\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Merhamet, yeni bir başlangıçtan sonra henüz sağlam yürüyemeyenin kırılganlığını onaran doğum sonrası bakım boyutuna da taşınabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_000552/B003","root_001064/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e76643a9ca3019e16e68","text":"2. **The doubled mercy as protected formation and continuing support.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ر ح م` B003 contributes the womb as a vessel of growth, while B001 contributes active beneficence. Before it, `ر ب ب` B002 supplies stagewise nurture; after it, `ع و ن` B001, `ه د ي` B001, and `ن ع م` B001 supply help, guidance, and bestowed favor. **Structural cue:** dependent-making actions extend on both sides of the repeated pair. **Role and abductive move:** the womb is the enclosure model and the surrounding acts are the developmental process; I infer that mercy both contains and sustains that process. **Reading change:** the two forms can carry complementary spatial and temporal force: encompassing protection and care that continues through the dependent’s movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
